Jade combat implementation audit

2026-11-02 · commit 0a1d42147efc4220ba49a2fa6a963ff3a77b0190

Describe current implementation, not certify game correctness. Review against normal datamine plus explicit user-confirmed mechanics. No combat data changed by this report.

Complete machine-readable report and per-hit trace

Review priorities | Formula | Mechanics | All skill records | Bonus definitions | Reference run | Coverage gaps

Review priorities

These are review questions, not approved corrections. Source behavior must be established before changing combat data or accepting new DPS snapshots.

J01 — Combo tier replacement versus addition [High]

Observed: The BT19 reference hit state contains hpDMGBonus 0.1 AND 0.2 for Ballistic on JadeCombo. The damage resolver adds both (30%).

Verify: Compare base Combo and Blossom Barrage T1/T6 source effects. Is the upgraded value 20% TOTAL or +20 percentage points? Do not assume modify.effect replaces the base array.

data/debuff/silkbind-jade.json#/JadeCombo · data/innerway/blossom-barrage.json#/effect/BlossomBarrageT1 · src/calculations/damage.ts

J02 — Ordinary Star Reacher replacement versus addition [High]

Observed: The BT19 reference state contains physicalAttackBonus 0.05 AND 0.075 on StarReacherBone, giving 12.5% before other physical attack bonuses. Enhanced StarReacherBoneEnhanced carries 15%.

Verify: Verify whether T1 should replace 5% with 7.5%, rather than add it. Check shared effect-definition modifier semantics and source ownership.

data/buff/silkbind-jade.json#/StarReacherBone · data/innerway/star-reacher.json#/effect/StarReacherT1

J03 — Star Reacher enhanced branches and T6 eligibility [High]

Observed: StarReacherGrant selects the same 15% enhanced buff for Exhausted OR Qi below 30%; the T6 3% damage rule uses LingeringBone OR Airborne plus HP >75%.

Verify: Compare raw source conditions for the exported 15%/25% wording and whether T6 requires LB AND Airborne rather than OR. Translation alone is insufficient.

data/skill/inkwell-fan.json#/StarReacherGrant · data/innerway/star-reacher.json

J04 — UF separate hits and resource feedback [High]

Observed: One / two / three separate damage actions for ordinary / LB / Airborne volleys. Each qualifying exhausted hit refunds 3 Blossoms. LB is refreshed once per volley after damage.

Verify: Separate hits and individual refunds were explicitly confirmed by the user on 2026-11-02. Verify per-hit event identity, any sub-hit timing, and which listeners have cooldowns; do not recombine multiplied coefficients.

data/skill/vernal-umbrella.json#/UnfadingFlowerProjectile · data/martial-art/vernal-umbrella.json · data/buff/silkbind-jade.json#/UnfadingFlowerMode

J05 — Spring Away Blossom Barrage T4 scope [Medium]

Observed: The current T4 damage/Combo-extension conditions explicitly require UnfadingFlowerProjectile. Spring Away uses SpringAwayProjectile.

Verify: The exported T4 text also mentions Spring Away. Verify raw conditions before extending the existing mechanism.

data/innerway/blossom-barrage.json#/effect/BlossomBarrageT4 · data/skill/vernal-umbrella.json#/SpringAwayProjectile

J06 — Golden Body support is partial [Medium]

Observed: Zero-time cancel, delayed Shield application at +0.5s, ten-second Shield and twenty-second cooldown. No numerical absorption, shield break from incoming damage, Vitality cost, defensive bonuses or expiry healing.

Verify: Cancel/application timings are user-confirmed. Review shield survival assumptions for Rain Whisper; do not treat this as a full defensive implementation.

data/skill/mystic.json#/GoldenBodyCancel · data/buff/general.json#/Shield

J07 — Nominal timing and distance [Medium]

Observed: UF first callback +1.15s from activation start, then every 0.5s. Spring Away uses nominal projectile timing; Peak moves to 1m before its main hit in flying presets.

Verify: Distinguish cast/input, launch/request, collider opening and real impact. Travel and contact uncertainty is not eliminated by using the nominal export.

doc/skill-data.md · data/rotation/silkbind-jade/jade-flying.json

J08 — Mystic enlightenment provenance [Medium]

Observed: Smolder, Dragon Head - Tide and Ghostly Umbra use established enlightenment routes; regular exported curves are not substitutes. Offensive Mystic coefficients use level 76.

Verify: Reconstruct each multiplier once. Preserve Smolder user-confirmed 0.0532 tick factor; investigate its conflict with exported 0.045 separately.

doc/skill-data.md · data/skill/mystic.json · data/dot/mystic.json

J09 — Snapshot comparison and stale prose [Medium]

Observed: Older sections of skill-data.md describe the obsolete 60s IV preset, seven cycles, unavailable pursuit timing and old default builds.

Verify: Use current JSON and the reference runtime, not historical prose, as the implementation baseline. Screenshot 75 UF attempts and 117255 DPS are external observations with unknown complete gear/outcome state.

doc/skill-data.md · data/path.json · data/build/silkbind-jade

J10 — Dodge semantics [Medium]

Observed: UF heavy has no projectile attunement. Soaring 0.5s, Phoenix/Fan Q 0.4s windows require incoming attacks. Overlap awards only latest active response; explicit Perfect Dodge/successful Deflect retain legacy no-attack fallback.

Verify: Verify window start/end boundaries, cancellation persistence, same-timestamp paired attacks and fallback semantics independently.

src/calculations/rotationTimeline.ts · data/skill/general.json · tests/attack-response.test.ts

Formula and evaluation semantics

baseline

BasePhysical = phyCoef × (outcomePhysicalAttack − adjustedEnemyDefense) + phyBonus × (1 + eligibleFlatAttackBonus). BaseAttribute[channel] = attrCoef × outcomeAttributeAttack[channel] + primaryPathOnly(attrBonus × (1 + eligibleFlatAttackBonus)). Honor any coefficient modifiers before these expressions.

channels

Physical clamps to zero after multiplication. Physical and each of four attribute channels use their own penetration/resistance and channel-damage multiplier. Primary Silkbind path multiplier is 1.5; other attribute paths use 1.

shared

action.damageScale (default 1) × (1 + sum(baseDMGBonus)) × (1 + sum(Category1)) × (1 + sum(matchingAttunementDMGBonus)); then channel-specific mechanism multiplier and outcome multiplier. Category1 sums eligible gear bonuses, dmgBonus and hpDMGBonus; it is not a product of each named buff. Attribute channels add attributeDMGBonus inside their Category1 bracket. Physical/attribute channel stat damage bonuses multiply separately. physicalAttackBonus modifies the effective attack range, not final damage.

outcomes

Expected damage weights Abrasion/Normal/Critical/Affinity using shared effective rates. Critical multiplier clamp(1 + effectiveCritDmgBonus + activeCritDmgBonus,1.35,2.50); Affinity clamp(...,1.20,2.30). Simulation samples outcomes and attack ranges; expected/sampled must share mechanics.

mechanism

1 + globalDmgBonus + globalHPDMGBonus; Bellstrike additionally includes globalBellstrikeDMGBonus. Exhausted currently uses +0.1 globalDmgBonus. It is not an additive Category1 bonus.

attunement

All top-level effect.tags must match; each nested array is OR. Excluded tags must not match. One definition contributes once even if multiple OR alternatives match. Amounts come from equipped gear, not the multiplier 1 in the definition.

time

Action times are relative to owning cast/component start; source actions can outlive cast completion. Damage resolves in event order using hit-time state and explicit snapshot rules. Array order resolves ties. BattleEnd excludes hits at its timestamp. Skill grouping changes reporting only.

statPipeline

Use shared characterComposition/characterStats/rotationCalculationBundle, not sums of displayed numbers. InnerWay bySoloLevel tables use solo level; martialArt talent[rank] is a complete rank, not sum of ranks. BT17/18/19 use martial rank13/14/15. Raw-stat-derived conversions and effective attack/outcome calculations remain in the shared pipeline.

Authoritative formula detail: damage-formula.md; engine: damage.ts, rotationTimeline.ts.

Mechanics summary

Unfading Flower

Activation normal 1.093s or cancel 0.6s; initial heavy at 0.5963992753846155s, mode at 0.6s. The heavy is Special-tagged in current data, not Heavy-tagged despite its reporting category, and has no FrequentProjectile tag. Requires >=50 Blossoms and inactive UF. Mode blocks ordered Umbrella skills, not triggered projectiles. Starts from 100 in presets. Base drain 10/s; LB adds 10/s; Airborne adds 15/s instead. No passive 0.3/s regen in mode. Depletion ends mode immediately; 15 Blossoms return after 0.2s. Volley first +0.55s after mode, interval 0.5s. Each projectile is Cp=Ca=0.51082875, Bp=141.375, Ba=77, plus 15% PvE HP damage; LB gives 2 hits and Airborne 3.

Spring Sorrow

Two base charges; Blossom Barrage T0 allows three; recharge sequentially at 15s. T3 selects faster Barrage component. One damage hit, then T6 checks existing Combo for +25 Blossoms and -5s cooldown; baseline +20, then applies Combo. That ordering means the first Q on an unmarked target does not get the existing-Combo refund.

Heavy / Colorful Phoenix

VernalHeavy grants 5 Blossoms on its hit. Colorful variants expose one, two or three hits; fixed +4 grants from the target-check chain no longer stop at 100, while the shared resource cap clips the balance. Read every timed action, not just damage rows, for full Blossom generation.

Spring Away

Editable channel hold capped at 10s, plus 0.74s startup and 0.5909090909090908s recovery. Endurance depletion ends channel early; launched projectiles survive recovery. Channel drains 10 Endurance/s before cost modifiers. Each projectile restores 3.4 Blossoms and has 25% PvE HP damage. It is Charged and FrequentProjectile, so Startling Spring and the selected projectile attunement can apply.

Emerald Barrier

Two sequential 15s charges shared by full/cancel. Damage at 0.15006s; Jadebreak at 0.167s. Full cast ends and grants Windrider at 1.02s; cancel ends at 0.167s without Windrider. Jadebreak gives Ballistic +30% plus another +10% to Ballistic OR Pursuit. These are additive Category 1 effects.

Forsaken Fame

One Fan charged-light hit at 1.19s, full cast 1.63s; applies LB and restores 10 Endurance. Intrinsic +45% HP damage is a modifier, not included in coefficients. Charged enables Startling Spring; applying LB enables Star Reacher through its damage trigger.

Moon Shatter Spring

Requires LB or Airborne; six-second cooldown. Windrider selects the five-hit branch at cast start; otherwise three hits. Nominal interrupt 2.433s / 1.557s. NPC +45% is already in the hit coefficients: do not add it again. Each hit adds Startling Spring. Exact per-branch actions and requirements are in the skill records.

Peak’s Springless Silence

Full 1.1133333333333333s; cancel 0.557581475s and must lead into pursuit. Preliminary contact at 0.35s is 0.5% of damage and requires distance <2.5 and not Airborne; main contact at 0.557581475s carries 99.5%. Existing LB/Airborne gates Windrider; LB grant is not equivalent to having a pre-existing mark. Shared twelve-second cooldown.

Drunken Poet / Smolder

Poet composite variants execute selected stages; Intoxicated gates their routes. Each strike can trigger its own Combustion or Smolder explosion. All are reported as Drunken Poet, including explosions. Fifth-hit enhancement consumes stacks and uses the authored modifier snapshot. Smolder hits, DOT ticks, and Poet explosions are distinct events. Cancel variants require the accepted follow-up.

Flute / Ghostly Umbra / Dragon Head

Flute damage bonus reads per-hit distance: 2% at 1m, 20% at 9–<20m. Ghostly Umbra damage boost and counterattack require successful dodge. Dragon Head - Tide uses eight authored Surging Waves stacks; during Exhausted they provide a separate base-damage multiplier as well as Category 1 damage. Its missing-HP bonus is hit-time; do not replace its enlightenment coefficients with regular Dragon Head.

Build and encounter

Short Fight = Rain Whisper + Breaking Point; Long Fight = Mistwillow + Thunderous Bloom. Both use Blossom Barrage, Morale Chant, Star Reacher. Max Short Fight uses one Precision and nine Crit rolls, retains Power, and tier100 All Mystic on helmet/chest. Rain Whisper grants +10% crit damage, plus +15% while Shield exists. 30s dummy has incoming paired attacks at 5.5+6n seconds; Qi is explicitly authored, not inferred from damage.

Every implemented Jade weapon skill and shared Mystic

Damage tables show unmodified authored numbers. Conditional rows are alternatives or additional hits according to their requirements; never sum them without evaluating those requirements. Composite parents may have no direct damage. Expanded JSON contains all subactions, timings, modifiers, resource actions, requirements and tags. Potential bonus sources are tag-compatible candidates only: they require their own tier, activation, stack and target conditions. Shared Mystic records not selected by Jade presets remain available for filler review.

Rank tables are alternatives, not cumulative. Inner Way tiers are composed by the shared pipeline. In particular, inspect effect-definition modifications rather than assuming an upgraded effect replaces its original.

All 191 damage action records, including hit-time buffs/debuffs and outcome-weighted damage, are in referenceCalculation.hits. Time is relative to battle start; effect appliedAt/expiresAt fields retain the engine absolute clock (subtract referenceCalculation.anchorTime to compare).

Unmapped export candidates and handoff procedure

The runtime weapon catalog has 25 records. This is not a claim that every in-game light/heavy stage or variant is implemented. The following source IDs were selected by weapon-ID prefix and lack a confirmed mapping here; some may be internal helpers or alternate routes.

  1. Match each runtime ID to normal-variant source ability, calcpoint and numerical owner. Record source hashes, layer, route, level and coefficient multiplier.
  2. Separate input, animation, launch and contact clocks. Check cancellation and after-cast actions independently.
  3. For every bonus, identify additive pool versus independent multiplier, skill-tag scope, target/HP/Qi conditions, stacks, refresh/extension and cooldown.
  4. Resolve J01–J05 against raw consumers. Do not normalize the result to match the screenshot.
  5. Reproduce the reference run through buildPresetRotationBundle and calculateRotationBaseline. Verify hit counts, resource refunds and buff state before comparing DPS.
  6. Return findings as runtime ID + source pointer + evidence + current behavior + proposed correction + affected tests/presets. Repository AGENTS.md requires approval of changed DPS snapshots before refreshing them.
